Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to issue an
appropriate Writ order or direction more particularly in the nature of Writ of Mandamus
declaring the impugned circular bearing No.Unit V(1)349/2002 dated 8-7-2002 issued by the
Ist respondent herein as arbitrary, illegal, capricious and violative of all cannons of law and
provisions of the AP Markets Act 1966 and consequently quash the same.
Counsel for the Petitioners:MR.SAI GANGADHAR CHAMARTY
Counsel for Respondent Nos.1 and 2: G.P.FOR AGRICULTURE
Counsel for Respondent Nos. 3 to 13: MR.K.MADHAVA REDDY
(Standing Counsel for Agricultural Market Committees)
WRIT PETITION NO : 12968 of 2003

B.R.K.R.Buildings, Hyderabad.

West Godavari District.
.....RESPONDENTS

Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a Writ of Mandamus, or any other appropriate writ, order or direction the action
of the 2nd respondent in levying market fee on the sales effected by the petitioners
during the financial year 2002-2003 vide impugned Notice No.351/2002 dated 23-5-
2003 as arbitrary, whimsical, unauthorised, ultra virus of section 12 of the Andhra
Pradesh (Agricultural Produce and live stock) Market Act, 1966 and contrary to the
Rules framed thereunder and set aside the same as such and pass such other order
or orders.
Counsel for the Petitioner:MR.P.BALAJI VARMA
Counsel for Respondent No.1: GP FOR AGRICULTURE
Counsel for Respondent No.2:MR.K.MADHAVA REDDY
(Standing Counsel for Agricultural Market Committees)

.....RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a Writ of Mandamus, or any other appropriate writ, order or direction, declaring
the action of the 2nd Respondent in levying market fee on the sales effected by the
petitioners during the financial year 2002-2003 vide impugned Notice
No.26/B/A/2002 dated 9-6-2003 as arbitrary, whimsical, unauthorised, ultra virus of
Section 12 of the Andhra Pradesh (Agricultural Produce and Live stock) Market Act,
1966 and contrary to the Rules framed thereunder and set aside the same as such
and pass.
Counsel for the Petitioner:MR.P.BALAJI VARMA
Counsel for Respondent No.1: GP FOR AGRICULTURE
Counsel for Respondent No.2: MR.K.MADHAVA REDDY
(Standing Counsel for Agricultural Market Committees)

circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue an appropriate Writ, Order or direction more particularly one in the nature of
Writ of Mandamus declaring that Item-2 of Schedule-II of the A.P. (Agricultural

Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a Writ of Mandamus or any other appropriate Writ declaring that the action of
the respondents in notifying the Rice as agricultural produce for the purpose of levy
of Market Fee under the provisions of the A.P. Agricultural (Produce & Live Stock)
Markets Act, 1966, is arbitrary and illegal, and direct the respondents not to insist the
petitioners to pay the Market Fee on the Rice Milled from out of Paddy, if necessary
by granting a declaring that Rice is not an agricultural produce within the meaning of
the A.P. Agricultural (Produce & Live Stock) Markets Act, 1966, and grant such othe
relief as it deems fit in the circumstances of the case.

Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to issue a
Writ of Mandamus or any other appropriate Writ declaring that the action of the respondents

in notifying the rice as agricultural produce for the purpose of levy of Market Fee under the
provisions of the A.P. Agricultural (produce & live Stock) Market Act, 1966, is arbitrary and
illegal, and direct the respondents not to insist the petitioners to pay the Market Fee on the
Rice Milled from out of Paddy, if necessary by granting a declaration that Rice is not an
agricultural produce withing the meaning of the A.p. Agricultural (Produce & Live Stock)
Markets Act, 1966, and grant such other relief as it deems fit in the circumstances of the
case.

Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a writ, direction or order, more particularly one in the nature of writ of
Mandamus, declaring the rule 48 of the A.P.(Agricultural produce and Livestock)
Markets Rules, 1969, as inserted by G.O.Ms.NO.459 Agriculture and cooperation
(MKTG.II), dated 23-12-2002 in so far as fixing and collecting of the licence fee for
minimum period of five years contrary to annual fees contempleated by section 33
(2) (VIII) of the A.P.(Agricultural produce and livestock) Markets Act, 1966, as illegal,
unconstitutional and ultravires under Article 14, 19 and 21 of constitution of India.

.....RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a writ, order or direction, more particularly one in the nature of writ of
mandamus, declaring that the action of the respondents in imposing market cess two
times on the same commodity namely paddy purchased by the petitioners after
payment of the market cess, is illegal, contrary to law and is his by principles of
doctrine of double jeopardy besides being arbitrary and illegal and consequently
directing the respondents not to impose or collect market cess on paddy which is
already charged and paid for if necessary by granting a declaration that the paddy
purchased by the petitioners, which was already charged and paid for any market
cess and fee is not liable for further market fees and cess in the hands of the
petitioners under A.P.Agricultural (Produce & Live stock) Markets Act, 1966.

Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to issue a
writ, order or direction, more particularly one in the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring
that the action of the respondents in imposing market cess two times on the same
commodity namely paddy purchased by the petitioners after payment of the market cess,
is illegal, contrary to law and is hit by principles of doctrine of double jeopardy besides being
arbitrary and illegal and consequently directing the respondents not to impose or collect
market cess on paddy which is already charged and paid for, if necessary by granting a
declaration that the paddy purchased by the petitioners, which was already charged and
paid for, if necessary by granting a declaration that the paddy purchased by the petitioners,
wchich was already charged and paid for any market cess and fee is not liable for further
market fees and cess in the hands of the petitioners under A.P.Agricultural (Produce and
Live Stock) Market Act, 1966.

Counsel for Respondents No.1 to 4 : GP FOR AGRICULTURE
Counsel for Respondents No.5 & 6 : MR.K.MADHAVA REDDY (SC FOR
AGRL.MRKT.COMTTS)

Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a writ, order or direction more particularly one in the nature of writ of
mandamus; i) to declare that rice is not an agricultural produce and not come within
section 2(i) of A.P.(Agricultural Produce & Livestock) Markets Act, 1966 and strike
down item-2 of Schedule-II of A.P.,(Agricultural Produce & Livestock) Markets Act,
1966, as illegal, without power, arbitrary and violative of Articles 19(1)(g) and 301 of
Constitution of India; and ii)consequently to declare that stopping or otherwise
obstructing the movement of rice by the respondents at Check Posts or any other
place demanding and collecting market fee from the petitioners, as illegal, beyond
the scope of Rule 75 of A.P.(Agricultural) Produce and Livestock) Markets Rules,
1969; and violative of Article 19(1)(g) and 301 of the Constitution of India;

Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a Writ, Order or direction more particularly one in the nature of Writ of
Mandamus: i] to declare that rice is not an agricultural produce and not come within
Section 2[i] of A.P.[Agricultural Produce and Livestock] Marketing Act 1966 and
strike down item -2 of Schedule-IIof A.P.[Agricultural Produce and Livestock]
Markets Act,1966 as illegal, without power, arbitrary, and violative of Articles 19[1][g]
and 301 of Constitution of India; or to declare that demanding Market Fee from
Purchaser [Traders] on Rice which obtained from Paddy on which Market Fee was
paid, asillegal, without jurisdiction contrary to Rule 74 of the Andhra Pradesh
[Agricultural Produce and Live Stock]markets Rules, 1969 and violative of Articles
19[1][g] and Article 301 of Constitution of India. ii] consequently to declare that
stopping or otherwise obstructing the movement of rice by the Respondents at
Check posts or any other places demanding and collecting Market Fee from the
Petitioner, as illegal, beyond the scope of Rule 75 of A.P [Agricultural Produce and
Live Stock] Markets Rules, 1969 and violative of Articles 19[1][g] and 301 of the
Constitution of India.
Counsel for the Petitioner:MR.POTTIVENKATA RAMANA RAO
Counsel for Respondent Nos.1 and 2: G.P.FOR AGRICULTURE
Counsel for Respondent No. 3 : MR.K.MADHAVA REDDY
(Standing Counsel for Agricultural Market Committees)

Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a writ, order or direction, more particularly one in nature of writ of mandamus
declaring the action of the respondents in notifying the rice as agricultural produce
for the purpose of levy of market fee under the provisions of the A.P. Agricultural
(Produce & Live stock) Markets Act, 1966 and declare the letter of the respondents

in circular No.Unit-V(1)349/2002, dated 8-7-2002 as illegal, arbitrary and
consequently direct the respondents not to insist the petitioner members of the to
pay the market fee on the rice milled from out of paddy, if necessary by granting a
declaration that rice is not an agricultural produce within the meaning of the
A.P.Agricultural (Produce & livestock) Market Act 1966.

circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a writ of mandamus or any other appropriate writ declaring that the action of
the respondents in notifying the rice as Agricultural produce for the purpose of levy
of Market Fee under the provisions of the A.P.Agricultural (Produce & Live Stock)
Markets Act, 1966, is arbitrary and illegal, and direct the respondents not to insist the
petitioners to pay the market fee on the rice milled from out of paddy, if necessary by

the A.P.Agricultura (Produce & Live Stock) Market Act, 1966 and grant such other
relief

Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a Writ, order or direction more particularly one in the nature of Writ of
Mandamus declaring that "Rice" is not "notified agricultural produce" within the
meaning of A.P. (Agricultural produce and Live Stocks) Markets Act, 1966 and the
Collection of market fee from Rice Miller-sellers in respect of rice sold to their party

purchasers without notice, enquiry and on the basis of circular of second respondent
Unit NO. V(1) 349/2002 dated 08-07-2002 by issue of Check-posts for the said
purpose is illegal and without jurisdiction and pass

.....RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue a Writ of Mandamus or any other appropriate Writ declaring that the action of
the respondents in notifying the Rice as Agricultural produce for the purpose of levy
of Market Fee under the provisions of the A.P. Agricultural (Produce & Live Stock)
Markets Act, 1966, is arbitrary and illegal, and direct the respondents not to insist the
petitioners to pay the market fee on the rice milled from out of paddy, if necessary by
granting a declaration that Rice is not an agricultural produce within the meaning of
the A.P. Agricultural (Produce & Live Stock) Market Act, 1966, and grant such other
relief as it deems fit in the circumstances of the case.
Counsel for the Petitioner:MR.P.BALAJI VARMA
Counsel for Respondent Nos.1 & 2: GP FOR AGRICULTURE
Counsel for Respondent No.3: MR.K.MADHAVA REDDY
(Standing Counsel for Agricultural Market Committees)

circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
issue an appropriate Writ, order or direction more particularly one in the nature of
Writ of Mandamus declaring that Item-2 of Schedule-II of the A.P. (Agricultural
Produce and Livestock) Markets Act, 1966 is not in accordance with Section2(1) of
the said Act and is illegal, arbitrary and unjust and further declare that rice is not an
agricultural produce and consquently direct the respondents not to stop or otherwise
obstruct the movement of rice at the check posts or any other places for collection of
market fee from the petitioner.

.....RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of the constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the Affidavit filed herein the High Court may be pleased to
Issue a Writ of Mandamus or any other appropriate Writ declaring that the action of
the respondents in notifying the Rice as Agricultural Produce for the purpose of levy
of Market Fee under the Provisions of the A.P. Agricultural ( Produce & Live Stock)
Markets Act, 1966, is illegal and arbitrary, and direct the respondents not to insist the
petitioenrs to pay the market fee on the rice milled from out of paddy, necessary by
granting a declaration that Rice is not an agricultural produce within the meaning of
the A.P. Agricultural ( Produce & Live Stock) Markets Act, 1966, and grant such
other relief as it deems fit in the circumstances of the case.
Counsel for the Petitioners : MR.B.DEVANAND
Counsel for Respondent Nos.1 & 2: GP FOR AGRICULTURE
Counsel for Respondent No.3: MR.K.MADHAVA REDDY
(Standing Counsel for Agricultural Market Committees)

COMMON ORDER : (Per Hon’ble Bilal Nazki, ACJ)
It is submitted that this batch of writ petitions raise the same questions of law and
fact, which were decided by this Court in Writ Petition No.18977 of 2002 and batch.
The writ petitions are accordingly disposed of in terms of the order in writ petition
No.18977 of 2002 and batch. A copy of the order passed in Writ Petition No.18977
of 2002, dated 21.12.2004 shall be placed on each file of this batch of writ petitions.
_________________
BILAL NAZKI, ACJ
28
th April 2005.
_____________________
Dr. G.YETHIRAJULU, J
ajr

bundles after the signatures of their Lordships.
